TL;DR: 本文研究了大尺度环境对射电活动星系核（AGN）触发的影响，发现致密环境（如星系群）显著增强射电AGN活动，其空间密度和分数均高于场环境，并揭示了触发机制可能在z~1.5发生转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管射电AGN的触发与宿主恒星质量关系明确，但其受大尺度环境影响的规律及宇宙时序演化仍不清楚。

Method: 结合VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz巡天与COSMOS-Web星系/星系群样本，选取射电超量AGN，分别构建星系群和场环境下的射电光度函数（RLF）与射电AGN占比，并拟合其随环境、恒星质量、射电光度和红移的演化关系。

Result: 在固定恒星质量下，星系群中射电AGN的空间密度比场中高3–30倍、占比高2倍；所有红移区间内，射电AGN占比均在r ≤ 0.2R₂₀₀处达峰；致密环境的增强效应持续至z=3.5；暗示z~1.5可能存在触发机制转变。

Conclusion: 大尺度致密环境显著促进射电AGN活动，且该效应具有质量、光度和红移依赖性；z~1.5可能是由星系群内介质主导转向并合与结构增长主导的关键过渡期。

Abstract: Radio active galactic nuclei (AGN) are key tracers of kinetic feedback from supermassive black holes. Although the dependence of their triggering on host stellar mass is well known, the impact of the large-scale environment across cosmic time is poorly understood. We investigate the role of the environment in triggering radio AGN activity, while also studying the dependence on stellar mass ($10\leq\log M_{\ast}\,{\rm [M_{\odot}]}\leq11.4$), radio luminosity ($22\leq\log L_{1.4}\,{\rm [W\,Hz^{-1}]}\leq26$), and redshift ($0.1\leq z \leq 3.5$). We combine the VLA-COSMOS 3 GHz Large Project with the COSMOS-Web galaxy and group catalogs, selecting radio-excess AGN and building radio luminosity functions (RLF) and radio AGN fractions separately for sources in galaxy groups (124 radio AGN) and in the field (405 radio AGN), out of the total of 20203 galaxies and 1678 galaxy groups. We fit the RLF to derive the cosmic evolution of radio AGN in different environments, and we provide a new parametric description of the radio AGN fractions as a function of environment, $M_{\ast}$, $L_{1.4}$, and z. Besides confirming the known positive correlation of radio AGN fraction with host $M_{\ast}$ in both groups and in the field, more importantly we find that the activation of a radio AGN in groups is enhanced relative to the field by a factor of 3--30 in space density and of 2 in radio AGN fraction at fixed stellar mass; the enhancement is also a function of $M_{\ast}$, $L_{1.4}$, and z. Furthermore, in all z bins, the radio AGN fractions peak within $r \leq 0.2R_{200}$. Overall, dense environments enhance radio AGN triggering out to at least z=3.5. We suggest the existence of a possible transition in the dominant triggering mechanism of radio AGN activity around z~1.5, potentially related to the intragroup medium in the core of groups at lower z and to merger and structure growth at earlier cosmic times.

</details>


### [117] [NGC 4936: abundant multi-phase medium in the central weak X-ray cool core and optically dark H I clouds in the group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10721)
*Prathamesh Tamhane,Ming Sun,Hao Chen,Thomas Connor,Megan Donahue,Mark Voit,Mpati Ramatsoku,Kokoro Hosogi,Tim Edge*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对NGC 4936星系进行了多波段分析，发现其拥有大量分子气体但缺乏中性氢（H I），支持高温高压环境下冷气体以分子形式主导的理论；电离气体和尘埃丝状结构延伸远超分子盘，并与邻近伴星系相互作用；星系冷气体可能主要源于热气晕冷却凝结，而非外部吸积；系统处于多相冷凝临界状态，星系相互作用可能促进冷凝并塑造多相介质形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解弱冷核星系团中心星系中异常丰富的冷/温气体来源及其物理机制，特别是分子气体主导而H I缺失的原因，以及星系相互作用在其中的作用。

Method: 结合SOAR光学成像、MUSE光谱观测及多波段数据分析，测量气体质量、分布、运动学、恒星形成率与AGN活动，并重新计算热力学剖面（cooling time / free-fall time）。

Result: NGC 4936含约2×10⁸ M⊙分子气体，H₂/H I质量比>17；电离气体和尘埃延伸至~15 kpc，与伴星系投影距离仅~2.5 kpc；SFR为0.29 M⊙/yr；AGN在亚毫米与X射线波段呈变异性；热力学分析显示最内区t_cool/t_ff ~25，接近多相冷凝阈值；周围环境探测到20个孤立H I结构。

Conclusion: NGC 4936中心冷气体主要起源于热气晕的大气冷凝，星系相互作用起促发和塑形作用而非主要供体；该系统为研究冷核阈值附近多相气体形成与演化的关键案例。

Abstract: We present a multiwavelength analysis of NGC 4936, the central galaxy of a weak cool-core group hosting an unusually large cold/warm gas reservoir. NGC 4936 contains $\sim2\times10^8$ M$_\odot$ of molecular gas in a rotating disk of radius $\sim1$ kpc, but no detected H I, with a $3σ$ upper limit of $1.13\times10^7$ M$_\odot$ within $\sim2.5$ kpc. The resulting $H_2$/H I mass ratio $>17$ is consistent with a predominantly molecular medium in the high-pressure environments of central galaxies. SOAR imaging and MUSE spectroscopy reveal ionised gas extending to $\sim15$ kpc in a complex filamentary morphology that broadly follows the molecular gas kinematics while extending well beyond the CO disk. Dust filaments similarly extend to $\sim14$ kpc and appear to interact with a companion galaxy located only $\sim2.5$ kpc away in projection. NGC 4936 has a star formation rate of $0.29$ M$_\odot$ yr$^{-1}$, and its AGN shows sub-mm and X-ray variability. We favour atmospheric condensation as the primary origin of the molecular gas, although externally acquired gas may contribute. Our recalculated thermodynamic profiles reach $t_{\rm cool}/t_{\rm ff}\sim25$ in the innermost region, placing the system near the threshold for multiphase condensation. The ongoing interaction may perturb the atmosphere and promote condensation while shaping the morphology and kinematics of the multiphase medium, rather than directly supplying most of the central cold gas. We additionally detect 20 H I structures in the surrounding group environment, with masses of $\sim10^7$--$10^8$ M$_\odot$, including several with no obvious optical counterparts.

</details>


### [118] [First uGMRT Constraints on Rotation Measure in the Radio-Quiet Seyfert Galaxy NGC 4235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10726)
*Salmoli Ghosh,Preeti Kharb,Janhavi Baghel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用升级版巨米波射电望远镜（uGMRT）对射电宁静型赛弗特星系NGC 4235开展多频偏振观测，首次在低频段对其kpc尺度射电辐射的法拉第旋转特性进行约束，测得核区旋转量RM≈200 rad m⁻²，并据此推断出法拉第旋转介质平均电子密度约为3×10⁻² cm⁻³，支持其起源于弥散磁化包层或AGN驱动风。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展经典法拉第旋转诊断方法至低功率、射电宁静型活动星系核（AGN）领域，并探索uGMRT宽带偏振观测在研究此类AGN周围磁化环境中的潜力。

Method: 利用升级版巨米波射电望远镜（uGMRT）在两个频段（1.3 GHz和663 MHz）对NGC 4235进行多频偏振观测，分析其kpc尺度射电辐射的线偏振及法拉第旋转测量（RM），并结合RM估算法拉第旋转介质的电子密度。

Result: 首次在低频段获得射电宁静型AGN NGC 4235的RM约束（核区RM≈200 rad m⁻²）；探测到核区与延展瓣的线偏振辐射；推得法拉第旋转介质平均电子密度约3×10⁻² cm⁻³（假设扰动尺度0.3 pc、磁化区域尺寸1.3 kpc）。

Conclusion: 结果表明NGC 4235核区的法拉第旋转由同步辐射区外部的磁化等离子体引起，支持存在弥散磁化包层或AGN驱动风；该工作成功将法拉第旋转诊断拓展至射电宁静AGN，并验证了uGMRT宽带偏振观测的有效性。

Abstract: We present a multi-frequency polarimetric study of the radio-quiet Seyfert galaxy NGC 4235 using the upgraded Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope (uGMRT), aimed at constraining the Faraday rotation properties of its kpc-scale radio emission. This marks the first-ever analysis of polarization across two frequency bands of the uGMRT and the first constraints on rotation measure (RM) at lower frequencies for a weak radio source (17.1$\pm$1.7 mJy at 1.3 GHz, 27.7$\pm$2.8 mJy at 663 MHz). We detect linearly polarized radio emission associated with the `core' and the extended lobes of NGC 4235. Broadband polarimetric analysis yields an RM value of {$\sim200$ rad m$^{-2}$} in the `core', indicating Faraday rotation by magnetized plasma external to the synchrotron-emitting regions. Using these RM constraints, we estimate an average electron density of the Faraday-rotating medium of $\sim$3$\times10^{-2}$ cm$^{-3}$ for a characteristic perturbation scale of $\sim$0.3 parsec within an assumed magnetized region of size $\sim$1.3 kpc, consistent with a diffuse magnetized sheath or AGN-driven wind. These results extend classical Faraday rotation diagnostics to the low-power, radio-quiet AGN regime and demonstrate the potential of uGMRT broadband polarimetry for probing magnetized environments in and around such AGN.

</details>


### [119] [Pushing Variability Searches to Extreme Dwarf Galaxies with the Vera C. Rubin Observatory Data Preview 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10755)
*Fan Zou,W. N. Brandt,Elena Gallo,Franz E. Bauer,Angela Bongiorno,Colin J. Burke,Alister W. Graham,Hengxiao Guo,Lorena Hernández-García,Marcin Marculewicz,Dieu D. Nguyen,Swayamtrupta Panda,Alessandro Peca,Bindu Rani,Claudio Ricci,Ohad Shemmer,Wei Leong Tee,Zhibo Yu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Vera C. Rubin天文台DP2数据，在ELAIS-S1深场中首次系统搜寻极端矮星系（EDGs，M⋆<10⁸ M⊙）中的光变源，发现7个候选体，其中2个为已知AGN，3个更似超新星，2个性质不明；EDG光变比例约为6.8×10⁻⁴，与大质量星系相当或略低，验证了Rubin望远镜在中等质量黑洞普查中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端矮星系（EDGs）是搜寻吸积态中等质量黑洞（IMBHs）最未被充分探索的星系质量区间，亟需系统性光变巡天来识别潜在活动星系核（AGN）候选体。

Method: 基于Rubin天文台数据预览2（DP2）在ELAIS-S1深场的差分成像光变曲线，对6.2×10⁴个具有多波段归档信息的EDGs，应用单波段与交叉波段变异性统计方法进行筛选。

Result: 识别出7个光变EDG候选体（i < 24, z < 0.3）：2个已知AGN、3个光变特征符合超新星、2个性质不明；EDG光变分数为6.8_{-2.1}^{+3.1}×10⁻⁴，与质量更大的对照样本相比相当或至多低3倍。

Conclusion: 该概念验证研究表明，Rubin望远镜将能提供具有统计意义的变EDG样本，开启中等质量黑洞种群研究的新窗口；但需后续光谱观测确认红移与物理本质。

Abstract: Galaxies with stellar masses $M_\star<10^8~M_\odot$, hereafter referred to as extreme dwarf galaxies (EDGs), represent one of the least explored regimes in the search for accreting intermediate-mass black holes. Using the recent Vera C. Rubin Observatory Data Preview 2 (DP2; covering 2025-04-25 to 2026-01-06), we present the first systematic search for variability among EDGs in the ELAIS-S1 field, the most extensively observed LSST Deep-Drilling Field in DP2. Applying single-band and cross-band variability statistics to difference-imaging light curves for $6.2\times10^4$ EDGs with archival multiwavelength characterizations, we identify seven variable EDG candidates, all brighter than $i$-band magnitude of 24 and located within $z<0.3$. The two brightest candidates are previously known AGNs, three have light curves more consistent with supernovae, and two do not have a clear nature. The sample corresponds to an apparent EDG variable fraction of $6.8_{-2.1}^{+3.1}\times10^{-4}$ at $i$-band magnitudes brighter than 24 and is expected to consist primarily of AGNs and supernovae. Compared to a magnitude-matched massive-galaxy sample with the same variability selection applied, the EDG variable fraction is comparable or at most lower by up to a factor of three. This proof-of-concept study demonstrates that Rubin will identify a statistically meaningful sample of variable EDG candidates, opening a new regime for black-hole demographics. Spectroscopic follow-up will nevertheless be required to secure redshifts and confirm individual candidates.

</details>


### [120] [The progenitor galaxies of the intracluster light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10785)
*Rebecca J. Mayes,Facundo A. Gómez,Antonela Monachesi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Illustris-TNG100模拟研究了星系团和星系群中弥散恒星成分（ICL）及其最亮团星系（BCG）的前身星系性质，发现ICL主要来自更多、更小、已熄灭的星系，其前身性质受结构形成历史显著影响，表现出系统间多样性；而BCG则由更少、更重的前身星系并合形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前红移z=0下星系团/群中ICL与BCG的前身星系性质差异及其演化关联，理解ICL形成机制及与中心星系的共演化关系。

Method: 基于Illustris-TNG100流体动力学模拟，选取127个质量高于10^13 M⊙的星系团/群；采用Holmberg半径处26.5 mag/arcsec²面亮度阈值分离ICL与BCG；统计并比较二者前身星系的质量分布、数量、星系类型（如是否熄灭）及径向质量梯度。

Result: ICL比BCG拥有更多、更小、更高比例来自低质量及已熄灭星系的前身；ICL与BCG+ICL的异地物质平均质量径向梯度为负且随系统质量增大而增强；该梯度及ICL前身性质高度依赖于各系统的形成历史，同质量系统间存在显著多样性。

Conclusion: ICL与BCG虽共演化，但起源路径不同：ICL主要由大量中小质量星系剥离/并合贡献，受宿主结构形成历史强烈调制；BCG则由更少、更高质量前身主导增长；因此ICL前身性质不具备普适性，需逐系统分析。

Abstract: Intracluster Light (ICL) is a diffuse stellar component of galaxy groups and clusters, bound to the structure's potential rather than to individual galaxies and primarily formed through the stripping and mergers of intermediate-mass galaxies. Its formation and evolution is strongly linked to the central galaxies of groups and clusters, typically the Brightest Cluster Galaxies (BCGs) in clusters. To determine the properties of the progenitor galaxies of the ICL of clusters and groups at redshift z = 0 and compare them with the progenitors of their BCGs. We select 127 clusters and groups in the hydrodynamic Illustris-TNG100 simulation above a mass of 10^{13}~$M_{\odot}$. We divide the ICL from the BCG by applying a surface brightness cut at the Holmberg radius of 26.5 mag/arcsec^{-2}, where star particles within this radius are defined as attached to the BCG, and outside, the ICL. We then determine the masses and contributions of progenitor galaxies of the ICL and BCG. We find that the number of ICL progenitors increases with structure mass, and that the ICL has more progenitors than the BCG. The ICL has a larger percentage of material from low-mass galaxies. Quenched galaxies contributed a large fraction of the ICL, and this fraction increases with galaxy mass. The mean mass radial gradient of ex-situ material in structures for the ICL and the BCG+ICL is generally negative and increases with structure mass, indicating that material from more massive progenitors is found towards the centres of clusters. The mean mass radial gradient is significantly impacted by structure formation history. ICL progenitor properties are impacted by structure history and are therefore highly individual to each system. At a given structure mass, there is diversity in the properties of the progenitors that formed the ICL. Although the BCG and the ICL co-evolve, the BCG has fewer, more massive progenitors.

</details>


### [121] [Mass Proxy Quality of Halo Properties in IllustrisTNG and FLAMINGO Simulations: II. Galaxies, Stars, and Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10901)
*Eddie Aljamal,August E. Evrard,Arya Farahi,Dylan Nelson*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析IllustrisTNG、TNG-Cluster和FLAMINGO等宇宙学流体模拟，系统研究了星系群与星系团尺度暗晕中恒星、黑洞及卫星星系等成分的质量相关统计性质，包括标度关系、协方差、条件似然分布和质量代理质量（MPQ），发现总恒星质量是最优质量示踪器，而卫星速度弥散度表现出对天体物理复杂性不敏感的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系团内不同成分（恒星、黑洞、卫星星系）如何随暗晕质量演化，并评估其作为暗晕质量示踪器的可靠性，以支持观测宇宙学和未来巡天项目。

Method: 基于多个高分辨率宇宙学流体模拟（IllustrisTNG、TNG-Cluster、FLAMINGO），在R500c半径内统计分析总恒星质量、BCG恒星质量、弥散光质量、黑洞总质量、卫星数及卫星一维速度弥散等属性，计算其质量条件下的标度关系、内在弥散、协方差、条件似然分布及质量代理质量（MPQ），并比较不同模拟与红移（z=0, 0.5, 1, 2）间的一致性。

Result: 各模拟间归一化存在最多2倍差异，但多数属性的质量局域斜率、内在弥散和MPQ高度一致；总恒星质量是最佳质量代理（质量散射7–14%），其次为卫星数（9–18%）；ICL质量相关性稳健，具观测潜力；卫星速度弥散σ_sat,1D表现出质量无关的斜率与弥散，且相关结构中性，表明其对天体物理模型依赖性低。

Conclusion: 尽管不同模拟在绝对量级上存在差异，但关键星系团内禀属性的统计行为具有高度普适性，支持将总恒星质量和卫星速度弥散等作为稳健、低模型依赖的暗晕质量示踪器，尤其适用于未来大样本观测验证。

Abstract: We study the stellar, black hole, and satellite galaxy populations of group- and cluster-scale halos in the IllustrisTNG, TNG-Cluster, and FLAMINGO cosmological hydrodynamical simulations. We characterize the mass-conditioned scaling relations, covariance, conditional likelihoods, and mass proxy quality (MPQ) of integral properties within $R_{\rm 500c}$: total stellar mass ($M_{\star,\,\rm tot}$), brightest central galaxy stellar mass ($M_{\star,\,\rm BCG}$), intracluster light mass ($M_{\star,\,\rm ICL}$), total black hole mass ($M_{\rm BH}$), satellite occupation ($N_{\rm sat}$), and satellite velocity dispersion ($σ_{\rm sat,\,1D}$), at $z\in \{0,0.5,1,2\}$. While normalization differences of up to a factor of two emerge among simulations, many properties exhibit remarkably similar mass-localized slopes, intrinsic scatters, and MPQs across halo samples and redshifts. Correlations among stellar, satellite, and gas properties are generally consistent in sign and their dependence on halo mass and redshift, whereas those involving $M_{\rm BH}$ and $N_{\rm sat}$ show greater variation, particularly in TNG-Cluster. Conditional likelihoods are nearly lognormal for many properties, including $M_{\star,\,\rm tot}$, $N_{\rm sat}$, and $σ_{\rm sat,\,1D}$, while $M_{\star,\,\rm BCG}$ and $M_{\star,\,\rm ICL}$ are negatively skewed. Among the properties considered here, total stellar mass is the best mass proxy, with a halo mass scatter of $7$-$14\%$, followed by $N_{\rm sat}$ with a mass scatter of $9$-$18\%$. The consistent correlation behavior of $M_{\star,\,\rm ICL}$ highlights its potential for next-generation observational studies. Finally, $σ_{\rm sat,\,1D}$ exhibits highly consistent scaling, with mass-independent slope and scatter and a largely neutral correlation structure, suggesting that it is relatively insensitive to astrophysical complexity.

</details>


### [122] [The conditional colour-magnitude distribution: III. A study of galaxy colour- and luminosity-dependent counts-in-cells statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10936)
*Raymon Isaac,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过计数-单元（CIC）统计方法检验了条件色-星等分布（CCMD）模型对SDSS星系样本的描述能力，发现该模型对大多数星系子样本拟合良好，但在高光度样本的某些CIC统计（如空洞概率函数、5 Mpc/h单元）上存在偏差；该偏差难以通过剔除Sloan巨墙或引入速度偏差修正；进一步利用CCMD模拟分析指出，小尺度/低平均星系数单元的涨落受物质环境与晕占有分布双重影响，而大尺度/高平均星系数单元则主要由环境效应主导，并探讨了其对星系组装偏差研究的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验CCMD模型在更精细的统计量（counts-in-cells）下对星系-暗晕关联的刻画能力，并探究CIC统计中星系数涨落的物理起源及其对组装偏差研究的启示。

Method: 使用SDSS观测数据中16个不同光度和颜色子样本的counts-in-cells（CIC）统计（包括void probability function等），与CCMD模型预测及三种解析CIC模型进行对比；辅以CCMD模拟，分解小/大尺度单元中星系数涨落的贡献（环境 vs. 晕占有分布）；尝试通过剔除Sloan Great Wall或引入速度偏差来改善模型偏差。

Result: CCMD模型对多数子样本的CIC测量给出良好拟合；但在高光度子样本的void probability function和R=5 Mpc/h的CIC分布上存在系统性偏差，且该偏差无法被剔除Sloan Great Wall或引入速度偏差所缓解；模拟分析表明：小单元/低平均星系数时，涨落源于环境与晕占有分布共同作用；大单元/高平均星系数时，环境效应主导。

Conclusion: CCMD模型整体上能较好地再现SDSS的CIC统计，验证了其作为星系-暗晕连接模型的有效性；但其在特定高光度样本中的偏差提示当前模型在极端环境或晕内星系分布建模上仍有改进空间；CIC涨落的尺度依赖性分析为区分和理解星系组装偏差提供了新视角。

Abstract: The conditional colour-magnitude distribution (CCMD) model connects the galaxy colour-luminosity distribution with dark matter halo mass, which has been constrained in Xu et al. by jointly modelling the two-point correlation functions of a large galaxy sample of the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS) in fine bins of galaxy colour and luminosity. We test the CCMD model of the galaxy-halo connection using counts-in-cells (CIC) statistics by comparing CCMD model predictions with SDSS measurements for 16 subsamples of galaxies in various luminosity and colour bins. In addition, we compare SDSS measurements with three analytic models of CIC distribution. The CCMD model provides a good description of the SDSS CIC measurements for most subsamples. The exceptions are the void probability functions and CIC distributions with cell radius 5 Mpc/h for a few subsamples in the luminous bins, which are not well described by the analytic models, either. For these subsamples, in general, removing the Sloan Great Wall from SDSS or introducing velocity bias to the CCMD model does not resolve the disagreement. Finally, with the help of the CCMD mocks, we investigate the cause of the variations of the galaxy counts in cells. For small cells or cells with a low mean galaxy count, the variation reflects effects from fluctuations in both the matter environment and the halo occupation distribution, while for large cells or cells with a high mean galaxy count, the environment effect dominates. We discuss the implications of our work for investigating galaxy assembly bias.

</details>


### [123] [Central stellar depletion in the Draco dwarf from Subaru photometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10941)
*Eduardo Vitral,Kyosuke S. Sato,Jorge Peñarrubia,Sakurako Okamoto,Veronika Dornan*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Draco矮椭球星系的 Subaru 深度测光数据，利用傅里叶统计方法探测暗物质子晕对恒星表面密度场的影响，发现其中存在中心恒星耗尽与环状过剩的径向波纹结构，并在傅里叶空间中检测到信噪比为6.0的低频峰；同时αβγ模型拟合显示表面密度随半径减小而增加，表明中心耗尽可能反映真实物理结构而非仅由恒星拥挤造成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质子晕是标准宇宙学模型的基本预言，但其低质量群体仍难以被观测约束；近期研究表明子晕与矮椭球星系相互作用可在恒星表面密度场中引发大尺度非平衡涨落，可通过傅里叶统计识别。

Method: 将基于傅里叶的统计方法应用于Draco矮椭球星系的Subaru深度测光数据（包含约10^4颗解析恒星）；采用人工星测试为每颗星赋予权重，并纳入离散似然框架；对比核化的轴对称Plummer模型与αβγ模型进行表面密度拟合。

Result: 发现Draco存在中心恒星耗尽、外围环状过剩的径向波纹结构；傅里叶空间中出现S/N=6.0的低频峰；αβγ模型拟合显示dΣ/dR>0在>99.9%置信度下显著，即表面密度随半径减小而上升。

Conclusion: 这些特征尚不足以作为暗物质子晕的稳健证据，但提示矮星系中心恒星耗尽可能反映真实物理结构，而非仅由观测拥挤效应导致。

Abstract: Dark matter subhaloes are a fundamental prediction of the standard cosmological model, but their low-mass population remains difficult to constrain observationally. Recent work has shown that interactions between subhaloes and dwarf spheroidal galaxies can induce large-scale, out-of-equilibrium fluctuations in their stellar surface density fields, identifiable through Fourier-based statistics. We apply this formalism to deep Subaru photometry of the Draco dwarf, extending previous tests to $\gtrsim\mathcal{O}(10^{4})$ resolved stars. We use artificial-star tests to assign completeness weights to individual stars and incorporate them into a discrete likelihood-based framework. Relative to a cored axisymmetric Plummer model, Draco shows radial corrugations that appear in the filtered normalised residuals as a central stellar depletion surrounded by a ring-like excess. The same feature appears in Fourier space as a low-frequency peak with a signal-to-noise ratio ${\rm S/N}=6.0$. Independently, fitting the surface density $Σ$ with an $αβγ$ model yields ${\rm d}Σ/{\rm d}R>0$ at $>99.9\%$ confidence -- i.e., a profile that decreases towards the centre. Although these signatures cannot yet be regarded as robust evidence for dark subhaloes, they suggest that central stellar depletion in dwarf galaxies may reflect genuine physical structure rather than crowding alone.

</details>


### [124] [NGC 6426: Colour-Magnitude diagram and variable star population as indicators of metallicity, distance, and age](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11016)
*A. Arellano Ferro,R. Michel,D. Deras,M. Vaca Montejano,S. Muneer,I. Bustos Fierro*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对球状星团NGC 6426进行了新的VI CCD测光观测，确认了变星V3和V9的双模脉动性质，报告了一颗成员星异常造父变星V17，并利用可能的成员星构建了去混淆、微分消光修正的CMD，结合RR Lyrae星傅里叶分解与I波段周期-光度-金属丰度关系标定，以及基于Dartmouth等时线与哈勃深场CMD的贝叶斯拟合，独立测定了该星团的距离模数、金属丰度、红化值和年龄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定NGC 6426的恒星成员身份、变星性质及基本物理参数（如距离、金属丰度、年龄），以检验恒星演化理论并增进对古老星团的理解。

Method: VI波段CCD测光；变星成员判别与周期分析；RR Lyrae星V波段光变曲线傅里叶分解；I波段周期-光度-金属丰度关系标定距离与金属丰度；基于Dartmouth等时线与HST CMD的贝叶斯拟合。

Result: 确认V3、V9为双模RR Lyrae星；发现一颗成员星异常造父变星V17；获得去混淆、微分消光修正的CMD；给出基于RR Lyrae和贝叶斯等时线拟合两套独立的距离模数、金属丰度、红化值和年龄估计。

Conclusion: NGC 6426的多方法联合分析提升了其基本参数精度，V17的发现丰富了该星团变星种群认知，CMD与理论等时线的良好吻合支持当前恒星演化模型。

Abstract: We present new VI CCD photometry of the globular cluster NGC 6426. The membership status of all point sources and, in particular, of all the variables in the field of view is discussed, including four recently detected variables. The double mode nature of variables V3 and V9 is confirmed and their periods are calculated. The presence of a cluster member anomalous Cepheid (V17) is reported. Employing only likely members we produce a decontaminated colour-magnitude diagram (CMD) differentially dereddened, which enables us to compare it with theoretical predictions and to study the variable stars distribution. The mean cluster metallicity and distance were calculated via the RR Lyrae V-band light curve Fourier decomposition and an I-band calibration of the Period-Magnitude-Metallicity relation. Further, a comparison using a Bayesian approach between theoretical isochrones of Dartmouth Stellar Evolution Database (DSED), with a deep HST CMD, gave us independent estimates of distance modulus, metallicity, colour excess, and cluster age.

</details>


### [125] [The Binary Fraction of Milky Way Field Stars in DESI: Dependence on Chemical Abundance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11102)
*Tian Qiu,Wenting Wang,Sergey E. Koposov,Songting Li,Nathan Sandford,Ting S. Li,Joan Najita,Jessica Nicole Aguilar,Steven Ahlen,Davide Bianchi,David Brooks,Aurelio Carnero Rosell,Todd Claybaugh,Andrei Cuceu,Axel de la Macorra,Arjun Dey,Peter Doel,Jaime E. Forero-Romero,Enrique Gaztañaga,Satya Gontcho A Gontcho,Gaston Gutierrez,Stephanie Juneau,Robert Kehoe,Martin Landriau,Laurent Le Guillou,Aaron Meisner,Ramon Miquel,Enrique Paillas,Will Percival,Francisco Prada,Ignasi Pérez-Ràfols,Corentin Ravoux,Graziano Rossi,Eusebio Sanchez,Christoph Saulder,Edward Schlafly,David Schlegel,Michael Schubnell,Joseph Harry Silber,Małgorzata Siudek,Gregory Tarlé,Benjamin Alan Weaver*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI银河系巡天第二版数据，通过多历元视向速度变化的统计方法，研究了银河系场星主序转折星的双星比例fb，并发现fb主要随金属丰度[Fe/H]降低而升高，且与化学丰度（特别是[Fe/H]）密切相关，可能源于金属丰度依赖的气体冷却效率和碎裂过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双星系统携带了恒星形成物理条件及后续演化的重要信息，但其在银河系不同恒星种群中的分布规律尚不明确，需通过大样本观测约束双星比例及其环境依赖性。

Method: 基于多历元视向速度变化的统计方法，分析DESI银河系巡天第二版（DESI MW Survey DR2）中场主序转折星的数据，拟合并分离双星比例fb与[Fe/H]、[Mg/Fe]、角动量、银纬等参数的相关性。

Result: fb主要随[Fe/H]降低而显著升高；在[Fe/H] ≳ −1.3时，fb随[Mg/Fe]略有上升，更低[Fe/H]下无明显趋势；fb与角动量和银面高度的相关性基本可由其[Fe/H]分布解释；薄盘、厚盘、恒星晕和GSE在[Fe/H]–[Mg/Fe]重叠区无显著fb差异。

Conclusion: 双星比例的变化主要由化学丰度（尤其是[Fe/H]）主导，可能反映金属丰度对气体冷却与碎裂的影响；较弱的[Mg/Fe]依赖性或与早期、高湍流、富气环境下的恒星形成有关。

Abstract: Binary populations encode information about the physical conditions of star formation and the subsequent evolution of stellar populations. Adopting a statistical method based on multi-epoch line-of-sight velocity variations, we constrain the binary fraction, $f_b$, of field main-sequence turn-off stars in the Milky Way using data from the second data release of the DESI Milky Way Survey. $f_b$ is found to primarily increase with lower [Fe/H]. At $[\mathrm{Fe}/\mathrm{H}]\gtrsim -1.3$, $f_b$ shows a modest increase with increasing [Mg/Fe], whereas no clear trend is detected at lower [Fe/H]. The apparent variations of $f_b$ with angular momentum and Galactic height are largely explained by their underlying [Fe/H] distributions, while the remaining variations broadly follow differences in [Mg/Fe]. Within overlapping regions of the [Fe/H]--[Mg/Fe] plane, the thin disk, thick disk, stellar halo and GSE populations exhibit no significant differences in their binary fractions. Thus, our measurements indicate that the variation in $f_b$ is dominated by chemical abundance, which could be explained by the metallicity-dependent gas cooling efficiency and fragmentation. The weaker [Mg/Fe] dependence at fixed [Fe/H] might be associated with the formation conditions of stars, with higher [Mg/Fe] stars generally forming earlier under a more gas-rich and turbulent environment.

</details>


### [126] [Isotopic ratios as probes of star formation and the origin of molecular gas in the Central Molecular Zone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11541)
*Laura Colzi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文综述了对银河系中心分子区（CMZ）中分子云G+0.693−0.027的同位素研究，发现其存在低温宁静气体成分，并通过碳同位素比值推断CMZ可能由外部未充分核合成处理的气体补给。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMZ虽富含致密分子气体，但恒星形成率异常低；同位素比值可同时探查局部物理条件与气体核合成历史，有助于理解其低效恒星形成的成因及物质来源。

Method: 综合分析G+0.693−0.027云的氘丰度（D/H）和^{12}C/^13}C比值（来自HC_3N和HC_5N及其双取代^{13}C同位素），结合天体化学模型校正同位素分馏效应。

Result: D/H比显示存在T_kin≲30 K的冷、宁静气体组分；^{12}C/^13}C比值经校正后为~37–48，显著高于CMZ常用值~20。

Conclusion: CMZ中仍可维持早期恒星形成环境；较高的^{12}C/^13}C比支持银河系中心正被沿棒结构内流的、化学演化程度较低的外部气体所补给。

Abstract: The Central Molecular Zone (CMZ) contains most of the dense molecular gas in the Milky Way but forms stars at a surprisingly low rate. Isotopic ratios provide a unique tool to investigate both the local physical conditions and the nucleosynthetic history of this gas. I summarize recent isotopic studies toward the molecular cloud G$+$0.693$-$0.027. Deuterium fractionation (D/H ratios) reveals a cold ($T_{\rm kin}\lesssim30$ K), relatively quiescent gas component embedded within the otherwise warm and turbulent CMZ, indicating that the earliest stages of star formation can survive in this extreme environment. I also present new $^{12}$C/$^{13}$C measurements from HC$_3$N and HC$_5$N, including the first constraints from doubly substituted $^{13}$C isotopologues. After accounting for isotopic fractionation with astrochemical models, the inferred elemental ratio ($\sim37$--48) exceeds the canonical CMZ assumed value ($\sim$20), supporting a scenario in which the Galactic center is replenished by less chemically processed gas flowing inward along the Galactic bar.

</details>


### [127] [Measuring radial flows in disc galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11588)
*Yi He,Filippo Fraternali,Pavel E. Mancera Piña,Enrico M. Di Teodoro*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，利用3DBarolo软件结合自助法，可靠地测量盘状星系中的轴对称径向速度，以区分真实径向流与翘曲结构造成的速度场畸变；在TNG50模拟数据验证中，该方法有效解除了径向流与翘曲的简并，但未发现全局一致的径向流趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接观测星系从星系际介质吸积气体的证据缺乏，而径向流是连接外盘吸积气体与内盘恒星形成的潜在关键环节，亟需可靠探测和量化方法。

Method: 基于3DBarolo开发专用工作流，通过三维运动学拟合提取轴对称径向速度，并采用自助法（bootstrapping）评估不确定性；用理想化轴对称模拟星系及TNG50中类银河系‘观测’样本进行验证。

Result: 方法成功区分径向流与翘曲效应，在测试中克服了二者速度场简并；TNG50分析显示星系盘存在局域非轴对称流入/流出，但整体无显著全局径向流趋势。

Conclusion: 所提方法为探测和量化星系径向气体流提供了稳健工具，有助于未来借助观测数据反推吸积气体性质；但当前模拟结果不支持普遍存在大尺度相干径向流的图像。

Abstract: Numerous theoretical studies suggest that gas accretion from the intergalactic medium is essential to feed star formation in galaxies throughout cosmic time. However, the mechanism of gas accretion remains poorly understood, as direct evidence is lacking. In some models, gas accretion is expected to take place in the outer discs of galaxies, from where it should be transferred to the inner star-forming discs through radial flows. Finding such flows and quantifying them, therefore, opens the possibility of inferring the detailed properties of the accreting gas. Unfortunately, detecting radial flows is challenging due to the large disparity between radial and rotational velocities. Additionally, the distortions in velocity fields produced by these flows resemble the effects of warped structures, further complicating their quantification. To address this, we have developed a new methodology to measure axisymmetric radial velocities in disc galaxies. Relying on the 3D kinematic fitting software \texttt{$^{\rm 3D}$Barolo}, we have designed a dedicated workflow to measure axisymmetric radial motions in disc galaxies and to obtain robust uncertainty estimates through a bootstrapping approach. We validated this approach using realistic axisymmetric mock galaxies as well as a small sample of `observed' Milky Way-like galaxies drawn from the TNG50 hydrodynamic simulation. These tests demonstrate that our method works well and is capable of overcoming the degeneracy between radial motions and warps. The TNG50 galaxies we analysed have large regions in their discs with non-axisymmetric inflows and outflows, but they show, on average, no global trend of coherent radial flows.

</details>


### [128] [Binding Energies of Astrophysically Relevant Molecules on CO$_2$ Clusters: Benchmarking and the Donor-Acceptor Origin of Surface-Dependent Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12055)
*Aneesa Ahmad,Catherine Walsh,Stefan Vogt-Geisse*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT计算校准CCSD(T)/CBS方法，获得了12种分子在CO₂冰上的结合能分布，发现其结合强度普遍弱于水冰，且与氢键供体能力相关；结果表明CO₂冰中甲醛等分子更易低温脱附，可能影响冷相星际介质中气相分子丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天体化学模型多采用水冰测得的CO₂结合能，但缺乏CO₂冰上直接测量或计算的结合能数据，亟需准确参数以改进模型。

Method: 采用DFT方法并以CCSD(T)/CBS为基准进行校准，计算12种吸附质在含2–5个CO₂分子团簇上的结合能分布，并分析氢键作用机制及尺寸效应。

Result: 所有12种吸附质在CO₂冰上的结合能均低于水冰（比值0.66–0.92）；H₂CO在CO₂冰中脱附温度约57 K（水冰中为85 K）；不同吸附位点结合能差异超500 K；脱附峰温范围11–132 K；CO₂无法提供氢键可能导致H₂O:CO₂混合冰发生相分离。

Conclusion: CO₂冰的弱结合与受限氢键能力显著影响分子滞留与释放行为，应更新 astrochemical 模型中相关结合能参数，并考虑CO₂富集区域的独立演化。

Abstract: Carbon dioxide (CO$_2$) is among the most abundant interstellar ices and has been observed by JWST in water-rich mixtures and pure CO$_2$ environments. Astrochemical models adopt binding energies (BEs) measured on water ice because few BEs are available for CO$_2$ ice. We calculate BE distributions for twelve adsorbates (N$_2$, CH$_4$, HCl, C$_2$H$_2$, H$_2$S, HF, HCN, H$_2$CO, HCOOH, HNC, CH$_3$OH, H$_2$O) on CO$_2$ clusters containing two to five molecules using DFT calibrated against CCSD(T)/CBS, a coupled-cluster treatment extrapolated to the complete-basis-set limit. Binding strength depends on whether the adsorbate requires the surface to donate a hydrogen bond (H-bond). CO$_2$ can accept an H-bond but cannot donate one, and all six species compared with water ice from Ahmad et al. (2026) bind more weakly on CO$_2$. The ratio of the BE on CO$_2$ to water ice ranges from 0.66 for H$_2$CO and 0.75 for H$_2$O to 0.87 for CH$_3$OH, 0.89 for HCOOH and 0.92 for C$_2$H$_2$. Formaldehyde is predicted to desorb near 57 K from CO$_2$-rich ice, compared with 85 K from water-rich ice, and could contribute to cold gas-phase H$_2$CO. Although the mean binding energies are changing with cluster size for several species, the differences can be traced to local binding interactions likely to remain important in larger ice models. The BEs vary by more than 500 K between sites, while the desorption peaks range from 11 K for CH$_4$ to 132 K for HCOOH. The limited H-bonding capacity of CO$_2$ may favour separation of mixed H$_2$O:CO$_2$ ice into CO$_2$-rich domains.

</details>


### [129] [Too many or too bright? Status and perspectives in the study of the UV Luminosity Function at z>10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12121)
*A. Fontana,H. Atek,V. Bromm,A. J. Bunker,M. Castellano,P. Dayal,F. D'Eugenio,D. Elbaz,A. Ferrara,S. L. Finkelstein,S. Fujimoto,M. Giavalisco,Y. Harikane,C. Kobayashi,F. Pacucci,P. G. Pérez-González,P. Rosati,A. Trinca,R. Tripodi,P. Santini,E. Vanzella,A. Venditti,A. Weibel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文综述了詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）在z>9高红移星系搜寻方面的最新进展，重点分析紫外光度函数（UVLF）的演化，并对比理论模型；指出z≈14.44为当前确认最遥远星系的红shift极限，z>15候选体尚未被证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在早期宇宙（前5亿年）发现大量/高亮星系，远超预期，亟需新物理机制解释；同时z>15星系仍未被证认，需系统梳理现状与挑战。

Method: 对迄今全部JWST高红移（z>9）观测数据开展元分析（meta-analysis），构建平均化的紫外光度函数（UVLF）演化描述；并与多种引入新物理机制的理论模型进行系统比对。

Result: 确立z=14.44为当前光谱证认最遥远星系的红shift纪录；给出z>9 UVLF的统一演化趋势；揭示现有模型虽可拟合z≤14数据，但在z>15探测上仍无突破。

Conclusion: JWST已彻底革新高红移星系统计认知，但z>15探测面临根本性瓶颈；未来需更大面积、更深曝光及多波段协同巡天才能突破该红shift壁垒。

Abstract: The finding that galaxies in the first 500Myr were much more numerous and/or brighter than expected is often cited as one of the main discoveries of the James Webb Space Telescope in its first four years of operation. This result is now solidly based on an impressive number of surveys that have detected, spectroscopically confirmed, and characterized a relatively large sample of galaxies up to z=14.44, the redshift of the most distant galaxy currently known. This finding has motivated the introduction of additional physical mechanisms into theoretical models to reproduce the observed numbers and luminosities. These results contrast with the failure, despite ongoing efforts, to confirm any galaxy candidate beyond this redshift. This review summarizes the status of the search for and characterization of galaxies at z>9, focusing on the evolution of the UV Luminosity Function (UVLF), which is the main statistical description of the galaxy population that can be obtained at these redshifts. We first provide a comprehensive overview of the full set of JWST data obtained so far. We perform a 'meta--analysis' on the entire data set and provide a simple yet effective description of the global evolution of the UVLF, averaging over the various surveys. We also compare these results extensively with theoretical models that were developed to match the new JWST observations and that explore different physical mechanisms able to drive the observed evolution. We conclude with a summary of the contrasting results regarding the search for galaxy candidates at z>15, and outline the requirements for future surveys aimed at breaking this barrier.

</details>


### [130] [Modeling CO Line Luminosity Using FIRE Simulations: I. Methodology and Validation of Molecular Gas and CO Abundance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12159)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后处理流程，结合SKIRT和CLOUDY模拟预测FIRE-2星系中的CO谱线光度、分子氢质量和CO丰度，首次验证了该流程与观测的一致性，并强调金属丰度对CO光度建模的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建适用于强度映射（line intensity mapping）模拟的CO响应函数，需在高分辨率宇宙学模拟（如FIRE-2）中准确预测CO辐射；而现有方法难以兼顾物理真实性与计算效率，尤其在低质量及高红移星系中CO易被紫外辐射光解，需精确建模尘埃屏蔽效应。

Method: 开发一个后处理流水线：SKIRT计算每个气体元所受辐射场；CLOUDY基于五维参数网格（金属丰度、密度、湍流速度、辐射场、云尺度）查表并插值得到CO(1-0)光度L'_CO及H2和CO质量；所有计算在FIRE-2模拟数据上执行。

Result: 该流程成功复现银河系分子云的CO光度随AV~1起陡增的特征；在3倍太阳金属丰度下总光度与观测偏差<35%；金属丰度增3倍导致L'_CO变化达一个数量级；N_CO/N_H2比值在中等柱密度下与观测吻合（误差<0.3 dex），但在高柱密度下偏低；z=0时H2质量与xCOLD GASS观测一致（SFR≳0.3 M⊙/yr），但z≳1时模拟值低于观测（受选择效应与α_CO假设限制）。

Conclusion: 尘埃屏蔽对CO光解的金属丰度依赖性是准确建模CO辐射（尤其在低质量与高红移星系）的核心物理机制；本流程为后续CO强度映射模拟提供了可靠基础，但仍需在高红移和低SFR星系中进一步校准。

Abstract: We present a post-processing pipeline that predicts CO line luminosities, molecular hydrogen masses, and CO abundances for galaxies in the Feedback in Realistic Environments (FIRE-2) simulations, as a first step toward CO response functions for line intensity mapping mocks. SKIRT gives the radiation field reaching each gas cell. CLOUDY, tabulated on a five-parameter grid (metallicity, density, turbulent velocity, radiation field, cloud scale) and interpolated, gives $L'_{\rm CO(1-0)}$ and the $\rm H_2$ and CO masses. In this first of three papers we validate the pipeline against observations. For Milky Way (MW) molecular clouds, the predicted $L'_{\rm CO(1-0)}$ rises sharply at $A_V\sim1$ and matches the observed values above that column. For a uniform $3\,Z_\odot$ the summed luminosity is within 35\% of the observed total. A factor of three increase in metallicity changes the total $L'_{\rm CO(1-0)}$ by an order of magnitude. Along MW sight lines, the predicted $N_{\rm CO}/N_{\rm H_2}$ reproduces the observed upturn within 0.3 dex in $N_{\rm H_2}$ but falls below the data at higher columns. At $z=0$ our molecular gas masses agree with xCOLD GASS for ${\rm SFR}\gtrsim0.3\,M_\odot\,yr^{-1}$ and are converged across our simulation resolutions. At $z\gtrsim1$ observed gas masses exceed ours at fixed stellar mass, though selection effects and conversion-factor assumptions limit this comparison. CO luminosities are compared with extragalactic observations in Paper II. Capturing the metallicity-dependent dust shielding of CO from photodissociation is essential for modeling CO emission in low-mass and high-redshift galaxies.

*Callum T. Donnan,Mark Dickinson,Lorenzo Napolitano,Pablo Arrabal Haro,Anthony J. Taylor,Hollis B. Akins,Ryan Begley,Davide Bevacqua,Adam C. Carnall,Marco Castellano,Fergus Cullen,James S. Dunlop,Steven L. Finkelstein,Carter Flayhart,Mauro Giavalisco,Michaela Hirschmann,Ray A. Lucas,Derek J. McLeod,Ross J. McLure,Casey Papovich,Laura Pentericci,Borja Pérez-Díaz,Thomas M. Stanton,L. Y. Aaron Yung*

TL;DR: 本文利用CAPERS JWST/NIRSpec PRISM项目获得的183个具有可靠光谱红移的星系候选体，评估了z≥8.5高红移星系的光度选择方法，发现基于UV光度函数先验的p(z)选择法最有效，将确认成功率从约35%提升至约77%，同时保持更高完备性；共确认48个z≥8.5星系，其中最高红移源UDS-111893达z=12.98，无z>13源被确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确稳健地选择高红移（z≥8.5）星系候选体对统计分析（如紫外光度函数测量）至关重要，而现有多种选择方法需进行光谱验证与量化评估。

Method: 基于CAPERS项目中183个具有确凿光谱红移的候选体，系统评估多种选择标准（如探测滤光片信噪比SNR、Lyman-α断裂强度、低/高红移SED拟合的Δχ²），并引入基于紫外光度函数（UV LF）先验的p(z)选择方法进行对比。

Result: 发现UV LF先验法将z≥8.5确认率从~35%显著提升至~77%，且完备性优于保守的SNR和Δχ²截断；共确认48个z≥8.5星系，最高红移为z=12.98（UDS-111893），未发现z>13源。

Conclusion: JWST/NIRCam在z≥8.5的光度选择效果可通过光谱验证定量评估，而引入UV LF先验的p(z)选择法是最高效、平衡成功率与完备性的优选策略。

Abstract: JWST/NIRCam imaging has enabled efficient selection of a large number of galaxy candidates at very high redshifts. Accurate and robust selection is crucial for statistical analyses, such as measurement of the ultraviolet luminosity function (UV LF). A variety of selection methods and criteria have been proposed in the literature. We evaluate the selection methodology of galaxy candidates at $z\geq8.5$ using 183 candidates with secure spectroscopic redshifts obtained from the CAPERS JWST/NIRSpec PRISM program. The success of high-$z$ confirmation is strongly correlated with the photometric signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) in the detection filter, the strength of the Lyman-$α$ break, and the $Δχ^2$ between the best-fitting low-redshift and high-redshift spectral energy distribution model. However, the most efficient selection method is the use of a UV LF prior on the inferred $p(z)$ of a source, raising the success rate from $\simeq 35\%$ to $\simeq 77\%$ while achieving greater completeness than conservative SNR and $Δχ^2$ cuts. Out of the 183 candidates we spectroscopically confirm 48 sources at $z\geq8.5$ via detections of emission lines and/or the Lyman-$α$ break (particularly important for those with no emission line detection). We report the highest-redshift source in CAPERS as UDS-111893 at $z_{\mathrm{spec}}=12.98$, with no sources confirmed at $z>13$. This work provides spectroscopic quantification of JWST/NIRCam photometric selection at $z\geq8.5$, and demonstrates the effectiveness of a UV LF prior for high-redshift photometric selection.

</details>


### [132] [Inflow-driven galaxy evolution - II: A hierarchy in the scaling relations of star-forming galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12394)
*Kai Wang,Carlton Baugh,Sownak Bose,Shaun Cole,Carlos S. Frenk,Cedric Lacey,Julio F. Navarro,Kyle A. Oman,Amélie Saintonge,Tom Theuns*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过建模调控星系重子循环的气体流，揭示了恒星形成主序（SFMS）、气体-恒星质量关系和质量-金属丰度关系（MZR）三者之间的层级结构，并解释了它们的散射来源及高维相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系演化中重子循环如何塑造星系核心观测关系。

Method: 构建理想与宇宙学气体流模型，分析SFMS、气体-恒星质量关系和MZR的物理起源及其散射来源。

Result: 发现三者存在层级依赖：SFMS主要由宿主晕形成时间决定；气体-恒星质量关系继承其与恒星形成效率的散射；MZR则进一步依赖质量加载因子与演化状态。由此自然导出多个高维相关关系（如基本面、大小相关性等）。

Conclusion: 三个核心标度关系及其高维关联可统一归因于同一重子循环框架，为连接观测与物理机制提供基础。

Abstract: The star-forming main sequence (SFMS; $M_\star$--sSFR), the gas-to-stellar mass relation ($M_\star$--$M_{\rm gas}/M_\star$), and the mass--metallicity relation (MZR; $M_\star$--$Z_{\rm gas}$) are three core scaling relations for star-forming galaxies, and together they encode the information about the baryon cycle that operates during galaxy evolution. We investigate how these relations emerge by modelling the gas flows that regulate the baryon cycle, using both an ideal and a cosmological gas flow model, and identify a hierarchy among the three relations, with one further parameter entering the scatter budget at each step. In our model, the SFMS and its scatter are set primarily by the formation time of the host halo, with a weak dependence on the star formation efficiency and the mass-loading factor. The gas-to-stellar mass relation follows from the ratio of the sSFR to the star formation efficiency, and inherits the scatter of both. The MZR in turn follows from the gas-to-stellar mass relation together with the mass-loading factor, their relative contributions depending on the galaxy evolutionary state between the inflow-driven regime and equilibrium. This hierarchy naturally introduces further correlations among the three relations, such as the fundamental plane of star formation ($M_\star$--$M_{\rm gas}$--SFR), the fundamental metallicity relation ($M_\star$--SFR--$Z_{\rm gas}$) and its gaseous counterpart ($M_\star$--$M_{\rm gas}$--$Z_{\rm gas}$), and the correlations of each relation with galaxy size, which traces the star formation efficiency through the star formation law. Together, the three core scaling relations and the higher-dimensional correlations among them follow from a single framework, providing a foundation for linking the observables of star-forming galaxies to the underlying baryon cycle.

</details>


### [133] [ACORN I. Massive Black Hole Seeding and Tidal Disruption Events from Star Clusters in Cosmological Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12398)
*Yihao Zhou,Tiziana Di Matteo,Claire E. Williams,Aklant Kumar Bhowmick,Marta Reina-Campos,Carl L. Rodriguez,Elena González Prieto,Laura Blecha,Rupert Croft*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于星团中恒星并合形成大质量黑洞（MBH）种子的新模型，并将其应用于宇宙学模拟，显著提升了低质量星系中MBH-星系质量关系的物理性、MBH并合率及LISA探测事件率，并增强了早期MBH增长与可观测潮汐撕裂事件（TDEs）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在z≳6处发现大量高红移星团，为大质量黑洞种子形成提供了理想场所；而以往播种模型在低质量星系（M_galaxy~10^6–10^9 M⊙）中缺乏物理基础，难以解释观测到的MBH分布与演化。

Method: 构建基于星团内恒星并合的亚网格MBH播种模型：根据局地ISM生成亚网格星团，结合恒星演化、二体弛豫、金属丰度依赖的恒星风、潮汐撕裂事件（TDEs）及引力波反冲效应，在5σ过密区约束模拟中实现该模型。

Result: 生成质量为10^3–10^5.6 M⊙的MBH种子，种子丰度与质量由局地环境决定，改善了低质量星系的MBH–星系质量关系；单个晕内可原位形成多个MBH，使MBH并合率提升约100倍，显著增强LISA预期事件率；TDE主导10^3–10^4 M⊙ MBH早期增长，产生峰值光度~10^42–10^43 erg/s的明亮耀发；z≳8早期增长加速，至z=6时不同播种模型下中心MBH质量趋同于~10^8.6 M⊙。

Conclusion: 该物理驱动的星团并合播种模型更真实地刻画了高红移MBH种子的形成与早期演化，提升了对LISA、JWST及时域巡天可观测信号的理论预言能力，尤其适用于低质量星系和z>6宇宙。

Abstract: JWST has revealed massive star clusters at $z\gtrsim 6$, which provide promising environments for massive black hole (MBH) seed formation. We introduce a new MBH seeding model for cosmological simulations, physically motivated by the runaway collisions in star clusters. Subgrid star clusters are generated according to the local ISM and evolved through stellar evolution and two-body relaxation. The masses of MBH seeds formed through runaway collisions are determined by the properties of their host clusters, including the effects of metallicity-dependent stellar winds. We further follow tidal disruption events (TDEs) with the evolving clusters, which can be enhanced in MBH binaries, and account for gravitational-wave (GW) recoil following MBH mergers. We implement this model in a constrained simulation of a 5$σ$ overdense region, where seeds of $10^{3}-10^{5.6}\,M_\odot$ form in clusters with $10^{4}\leq m_{\rm SC}\leq 10^{8}\,M_\odot$. Because both the seed abundance and mass are directly connected to the local environment, we have a more physical $M_{\rm BH}\!-\!M_{\rm galaxy}$ scaling for low-mass galaxies ($M_{\rm galaxy}\sim10^{6-9}\,M_\odot$), a regime poorly described by previous seeding models. Multiple MBHs are naturally allowed to form in-situ within individual halos, producing a spatially clustered population. This increases the MBH merger rate by $\sim100$ relative to other seeding models, significantly enhancing the expected LISA event rate. TDEs can dominate the growth of $10^{3-4}\,M_\odot$ MBHs and produce luminous flares of $L_{\rm peak}\sim 10^{42-43}\,{\rm erg\,s^{-1}}$, increasing the observability of low-mass seeds. This seeding prescription accelerates early MBH growth at $z\gtrsim 8$, while subsequent self-regulated gas accretion drives the central MBH toward similar masses $\sim10^{8.6}\, M_\odot$ by $z=6$ across different seeding models.

</details>

*H. Athreya,J. Cornelison,M. Mikhailov,R. J. H. van der Kolk,C. Chang,T. Cecil,M. N. Ali,J. A. Zebrowski*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了在150°C低温下通过电感耦合等离子体化学气相沉积（ICPCVD）制备的氮化硅（SiNₓ）薄膜在超导探测器电路中的介电损耗特性，发现降低N₂/SiH₄前驱体流量比和施加射频衬底偏压均可有效降低介电损耗角正切，为温度受限的超导器件工艺提供了可调优的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波探测器电路中，介电损耗常是效率与谐振器性能的瓶颈；而某些优选探测器结构要求介电薄膜在远低于常规温度下沉积，因此亟需开发低温、低损耗的介电薄膜工艺。

Method: 采用ICPCVD在150°C沉积SiNₓ薄膜，利用超导铌微带谐振器在~4–7 GHz测量其介电损耗角正切，并通过拟合温度依赖的谐振频率至双能级系统（TLS）模型提取损耗；系统调控N₂/SiH₄流量比及是否施加RF衬底偏压。

Result: 发现介电损耗随N₂/SiH₄流量比降低而显著减小；施加RF衬底偏压进一步降低损耗；确认前驱体比例与衬底偏压是优化低温SiNₓ薄膜损耗的关键工艺参数。

Conclusion: 低温ICPCVD SiNₓ薄膜的介电损耗可通过调控N₂/SiH₄比例和引入RF衬底偏压有效改善，为温度敏感型超导探测器（如天文探测）提供了实用可行的低损耗介电薄膜制备方案。

Abstract: Low-loss dielectric films are an important component of superconducting circuits with applications ranging from quantum information to superconducting detectors for astrophysics. In millimeter-wave detector circuits, dielectric loss can be the limiting factor on efficiency and resonator performance. Some advantageous detector fabrication architectures require dielectric deposition at temperatures substantially lower than those conventionally used to achieve low dielectric loss. In this work, we characterize silicon nitride (SiN$_x$) films deposited by inductively coupled plasma chemical vapor deposition (ICPCVD) at $150\,^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ for use in temperature-constrained superconducting detector fabrication. We measure the dielectric loss tangent of these films at $\sim 4-7$ GHz using superconducting niobium microstrip resonators and extract the loss by fitting the temperature-dependent resonant frequency to a two-level-system model. We vary the N$_2$/SiH$_4$ precursor-flow ratio between dielectric depositions and find a clear trend of decreasing loss with decreasing N$_2$/SiH$_4$. We additionally find that applying an RF substrate bias during deposition reduces the loss compared to films deposited without bias. These results identify precursor ratio and substrate bias as practical process parameters for improving low-temperature SiN$_x$ for superconducting detector circuits.

</details>


### [135] [DeepDISC on LSST Data Preview 1: Scene-Level Detection, Deblending, and Star/Galaxy Classification in the ECDFS and EDFS Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10986)
*Prince Yadav,Yaswant Sai Ejjagiri,Xin Liu,Grant Merz,the LSST Dark Energy Science Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次将DeepDISC——一种场景级深度学习框架——应用于真实LSST DP1数据，实现端到端的源检测、分割与星系/恒星分类；通过在模拟数据上预训练并在真实DP1图像及光谱/Gaia标签上分阶段微调，显著提升源完备率，并在分类精度上接近现有随机森林方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天文学数据处理流程将检测、分割和分类作为独立步骤，效率低且误差累积；而LSST即将产生海量图像数据，亟需端到端、高精度、高完备率的自动化处理框架。

Method: 采用DeepDISC框架，在LSST模拟数据上预训练，再在DP1六波段拼接图像上用extendedness标志进行初步微调，最后基于光谱/Gaia/测光确认的恒星-星系样本单独微调分类头；评估采用类感知与类无关匹配、与Random Forest对比、以及与HST/CANDELS深场交叉验证。

Result: 微调后星系与恒星完备率分别达84.2%/49.1%（类感知）和84.9%/74.3%（类无关）；在共同测试源上分类准确率97.4%，略低于Random Forest的98.5%；对未见训练源的HST交叉验证显示其未识别混合源比例为14.9%，优于DP1官方目录的18.0%。

Conclusion: DeepDISC在真实LSST数据上展现出强大潜力，为后续自监督预训练及DP2数据处理奠定基础，是迈向全自动化、端到端天文图像分析的关键一步。

Abstract: We present the first application of DeepDISC, a scene-level deep-learning framework, to real LSST data, using the Data Preview 1 (DP1) coadded images of the Extended Chandra Deep Field-South and the Euclid Deep Field-South fields. In a single pass, DeepDISC jointly detects, segments, and classifies sources as stars or galaxies, rather than treating these tasks as separate steps as in traditional pipelines. We warm-start from a model pretrained on LSST simulations and fine-tune it on DP1 six-band images, using the DP1 catalog's extendedness flag (point-like versus extended) as provisional class labels. The classification part of the network is then fine-tuned alone on a curated sample of stars and galaxies with spectroscopic, Gaia, and photometric identifications, so the final output is a star/galaxy classification. Fine-tuning on real DP1 observations substantially improves source recovery relative to the un-fine-tuned model, raising galaxy and star completeness from 40.2% and 13% to 84.2% and 49.1% under strict class-aware matching; under class-agnostic matching the fine-tuned model reaches 84.9% and 74.3%. On a common sample of sources that neither classifier saw in training, DeepDISC classifies to within about one percentage point of a published photometry-based Random Forest for the same field (97.4% vs. 98.5%); at fainter magnitudes DeepDISC labels more sources as stars than the Random Forest, and independent labels favor the Random Forest in most of these disagreements. Cross-matching to deeper HST/CANDELS imaging on a held-out footprint gives an unrecognized-blend fraction for DeepDISC of 14.9% (95% CI 12.5-17.8%), consistent with or modestly below the 18.0% we measure for the production DP1 catalog. This work establishes a baseline for DeepDISC on real LSST data and a foundation for self-supervised pretraining and for LSST Data Preview 2.

</details>


### [136] [radio-astro-tools: linking radio astronomical data to the astronomical Python ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11429)
*Eric W. Koch,Adam Ginsburg,Thomas P. Robitaille,Erik W. Rosolowsky,Alyssa Bulatek,Savannah Gramze,Jonathan D. Henshaw,Derek Homeier,Preshanth Jagannathan,Amanda A. Kepley,Adam K. Leroy,Sébastien Maret,Stuart Mumford,George Privon,Álvaro Sánchez-Monge,Srikrishna Sekhar,Brigitta Sipőcz,A. J. M. Thomson*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了radio-astro-tools代码套件，一套专为射电数据（尤其是干涉仪光谱立方体）分析设计的Python工具包，兼容Astropy生态，并支持多波段通用分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决射电天文数据（特别是干涉仪光谱立方体）在Astropy生态中缺乏统一、高效、易用分析工具的问题，同时促进CASA与更广泛天文软件生态的互操作性。

Method: 开发了多个协同工作的Python包：spectral-cube（核心，用于读写与分析光谱立方体，支持dask/joblib并行）、casa-formats-io（读取CASA表和图像）、radio-beam（处理点扩散函数）、pvextractor（生成位置-速度图）、uvcombine（实现单天线与干涉数据‘羽化’融合）；同时向matplotlib、astropy、regions等上游库贡献了增强功能；配套提供交互式Jupyter教程。

Result: 构建了一个模块化、可扩展、跨波段适用的射电数据分析工具链，显著提升了光谱立方体处理效率与易用性，并加强了CASA与Astropy等主流天文软件生态的集成。

Conclusion: radio-astro-tools成功填补了射电天文数据在现代Python天文软件栈中的关键工具空白，推动了标准化、可复现与协作式数据分析实践。

Abstract: We present the radio-astro-tools code suite, which consists of several Python packages that enable analysis of radio data, especially interferometric spectral cubes, in the context of the Astropy software ecosystem. While these tools were designed with radio data in mind, they are built to be general and have applications on data sets at other wavelengths. The core package, spectral-cube, handles reading, writing and analysis of cube data, and it enables straightforward parallelization via dask and joblib backends. Support packages include casa-formats-io and radio-beam, which handle reading of CASA tables & images and reading and manipulation of point spread functions, respectively. The pvextractor package facilitates creation of position-velocity diagrams. The uvcombine package implements the "feather" algorithm for combining single-dish and interferometric data. The development of radio-astro-tools included several contributions to other repositories, including matplotlib, astropy, and regions to support interaction between CASA and other parts of the astronomy software ecosystem. We also present a detailed set of tutorials written in Jupyter notebooks that can be run interactively in a browser, providing more accessible access to data analysis for radio spectral-line data cubes.

</details>


### [137] [Pseudo Closed-Loop Bootstrapping for Reinforcement Learning Based AO Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11463)
*Eemil Halonen,Jalo Nousiainen,Markus Kasper,Nuutti Hyvönen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需初始稳定积分器控制器即可启动PO4AO强化学习控制策略的鲁棒预校准方法，通过内部光源和变形镜（DM）实现地面望远镜高对比度成像中强非线性波前传感器（WFS）下的高效波前控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性波前重构器难以应对高灵敏度波前传感器带来的强非线性问题，而现有基于强化学习的PO4AO方法依赖于积分器闭环采集数据，在强非线性或恶劣条件下难以启动，导致学习困难。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒学习策略：利用内部光源和变形镜对PO4AO进行离线预校准，绕过对初始稳定积分器闭环的依赖，实现直接上天闭环运行。

Result: 该策略使PO4AO可在强非线性WFS和挑战性条件下成功启动并学习鲁棒控制策略，无需依赖难以建立的初始积分器闭环。

Conclusion: 预校准策略显著提升了PO4AO在现实高非线性场景中的适用性和鲁棒性，为下一代地基高对比度成像系统提供了可行的智能波前控制新范式。

Abstract: High contrast imaging with ground-based telescopes requires extremely precise wavefront control. The performance of the controller ultimately depends on the quality and efficiency of the wavefront sensing technique, which has led to the development of ever more sensitive wavefront sensors (WFS). However, the increase in sensitivity comes at the cost of greater nonlinearity, which poses challenges for conventional linear reconstructors and controllers.
  Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a branch of machine learning in which control policies are learned by interacting with the environment. RL has recently attracted interest in the field of XAO, and previous studies have demonstrated that a model-based RL variant, the Policy Optimization for Adaptive Optics (PO4AO), can effectively compensate for temporal delays, misregistration errors, and moderate WFS nonlinearities. PO4AO learns an initial control strategy by collecting closed-loop data from the integrator controller with a linear reconstructor. However, in cases of highly non-linear WFS and challenging conditions, closing the loop with the integrator is difficult, and high-fidelity data cannot be collected. Consequently, learning robust control with PO4AO is challenging.
  We propose a robust learning strategy in which PO4AO is pre-calibrated using an internal light source and a DM, enabling direct on-sky closed-loop operation without a stable integrator controller for bootstrapping the initial models.

</details>


### [138] [A Coherent Harmonic Summing Pulsar Search Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11749)
*Scott M. Ransom*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoherentSearch.jl的新脉冲星搜索方法，通过直接从傅里叶变换重建脉冲轮廓，保留相位信息，在检测率、鲁棒性和计算效率方面均优于现有方法（如FFA和accelsearch）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脉冲星搜索方法（如功率叠加）丢弃相位信息，损失脉冲形状和部分信号；而折叠法虽保留信息但计算昂贵。本文旨在在不牺牲信息的前提下提升搜索效率与灵敏度。

Method: CoherentSearch.jl利用傅里叶谱的复振幅插值约60个谐波，逆变换生成脉冲轮廓，并用多宽度boxcar模板匹配；输入谱归一化使得噪声解析可得，从而实现全搜索统一阈值与恒定误报率。

Result: 在86万次注入测试中，该方法在白噪声下检出率76%，显著高于riptide（64%）和PRESTO accelsearch（42%）；对红噪声鲁棒；单核比riptide快1.4–2.2倍，48核扩展至27倍，GPU加速达20核工作站的9倍。

Conclusion: CoherentSearch.jl是一种高效、鲁棒、开源的相干脉冲星搜索工具，兼顾灵敏度、精度与可扩展性，适用于大规模巡天数据处理。

Abstract: Pulsars have narrow pulses, so their signals spread over many harmonics in a Fourier transform. Standard pulsar searches add the powers of those harmonics and discard their phases, which throws away the pulse shape and some of the signal. Folding the time series at each trial period keeps both, but is expensive even with the Fast Folding Algorithm (FFA). We describe CoherentSearch.jl, an open-source Julia code that reconstructs the pulse profile at every trial spin frequency directly from the Fourier transform. It interpolates the complex amplitudes of typically 60 harmonics, inverse transforms them into a profile, and tests that profile against boxcar templates of many widths. Because the input spectrum is normalized, the noise in every profile is known in closed form, so one threshold gives one false-alarm rate across the whole search. In an injection study of over 860,000 simulated pulsars at a matched false-alarm rate, the new search recovers 76% of the pulsars in white noise, against 64% for riptide's FFA in its recommended configuration and 42% for PRESTO's accelsearch, and its threshold does not move under red noise. On one CPU core it is 1.4-2.2 times faster than riptide at matched frequency coverage, it scales to 27 times faster on 48 cores, and modern GPUs run it up to nine times faster than a 20-core workstation.

</details>


### [139] [On-chip filled array hyperspectral kinetic inductance detectors (FHYKID) for line intensity mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12051)
*Emile Prêle,Martino Calvo,Usasi Chowdhury,Gregory Garde,Florence Levy-Bertrand,Alessandro Monfardini*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于下一代谱线强度映射（LIM）实验的高通量、高像素数超光谱填充式焦平面探测器FHYKID，工作于210–235 GHz频段，光谱分辨率达120，采用平面槽型分裂环谐振器（SSRR）与集总元件动能电感探测器（LEKID）耦合实现滤波与探测，具备高量子效率、结构鲁棒且可扩展至数万像素，将作为NIKONA仪器四通道之一部署于IRAM 30米望远镜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代谱线强度映射（LIM）实验需极高通量和大规模焦平面像素，尤其针对红移z=7–8的[CII]谱线观测，亟需发展新型高效、可扩展的毫米波超光谱探测技术。

Method: 设计并开发基于平面槽型分裂环谐振器（SSRR）滤波与集总元件动能电感探测器（LEKID）集成的超光谱填充焦平面FHYKID，工作频段210–235 GHz，光谱分辨率R≈120，利用其结构简单性实现高量子效率与可扩展性，并兼容现有读出电子学系统。

Result: 成功构建了FHYKID概念样机，验证其在目标频段内具备高光谱分辨率、高量子效率及可扩展至数万个探测单元的能力；该探测器已被选定为拟立项NIKONA仪器的四大通道之一，计划安装于IRAM 30米望远镜。

Conclusion: FHYKID是一种面向未来LIM实验、特别是高红移[CII]探测的可行且极具前景的技术路径，兼具性能、鲁棒性与工程可实现性，有望同时实现银河系/近邻星系CO谱线与宇宙学[CII]信号的联合测绘。

Abstract: The next generation Line Intensity Mapping (LIM) experiments will require very high throughput instrumentation, and as a consequence high pixel counts focal planes. For the study of the [CII] line in the redshift range z=7-8, we develop a hyperspectral filled focal plane (FHYKID) operating in the range 210-235 GHz and with a spectral resolution reaching 120. The spectral filtering is achieved thanks to planar Slot Split Ring Resonators (SSRR) coupled to Lumped Element Kinetic Inductance Detectors (LEKID). This robust concept achieves, thanks to its simplicity, large quantum efficiency and is scalable, even with current readout electronics systems, to several tens of thousands beams on Sky. Our FHYKID will be one of the four channels of the NIKONA instrument, currently seeking funding and to be installed at the IRAM 30-meter telescope at Pico Veleta. Among the science goals, simultaneously mapping the local (galactic and nearby-galaxies) CO line and the cosmological [CII].

</details>


### [140] [Opening the Black Box: What Neural Networks Learn from Pulsar Timing Array Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12308)
*James Alvey,Andrea Mitridate,Mauro Pieroni,Joseph D. Romano*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文使用简化的脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）玩具模型，对基于仿真的推断（SBI）方法在引力波背景各向异性检测中的应用进行了教学性解释，阐明了SBI相较于经典检测统计方法的提升条件与机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有及计划中的引力波实验在数据分析中面临诸多挑战，而基于仿真的推断（SBI）方法被提出以应对这些问题；特别是SBI分类已被证明可显著提升脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）数据中各向异性探测能力，但其原理和适用条件尚需更清晰的教学性阐释。

Method: 采用一个简化的脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）玩具模型，从教学角度系统分析SBI方法在引力波背景各向异性检测中的工作机制，并对比其与经典检测统计方法的性能差异。

Result: 明确了SBI方法在何种条件下能优于经典检测统计方法，并给出了其提升探测能力的物理与统计机制解释。

Conclusion: SBI方法在引力波背景各向异性探测中具有明确优势，其有效性依赖于具体模型设定与数据特征；该工作为后续将SBI应用于真实PTA数据分析提供了理论基础与教学指导。

Abstract: In recent years, simulation-based inference (SBI) methods have been proposed to address several data-analysis challenges faced by existing and planned gravitational-wave experiments. For example, SBI classification has recently been shown to significantly improve the prospects for detecting anisotropies in pulsar timing array (PTA) data. In this work, we use a simple toy model of a PTA to provide a more pedagogical explanation of how, and under which circumstances, SBI-based detection can improve on classical detection statistics for gravitational-wave background anisotropies.

</details>


### [141] [Data Representation Matters: Optimizing Machine Learning for X-ray Source Classification by Leveraging Spatio-Spectral Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12317)
*Maggie Lieu,Ivan Valtchanov,Marguerite Pierre,Esin Gulbahar,Elias Koulouridis,Iván Muñoz Rodríguez,Lucio Chiappetti*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文比较了不同CNN模型在XMM-Newton模拟数据上对AGN与星系团分类的性能，发现直接处理三维事件立方体（3D CNN）效果最稳健，并具备可解释性与端到端背景自动建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分微弱延展X射线源（如AGN与星系团）是X射线巡天中的基础但具挑战性的任务；现有方法依赖人工背景建模与多步处理，亟需更鲁棒、可解释、端到端的自动分类方案。

Method: 构建1D（光谱）、2D（图像）、2D+1D（联合）和3D（事件立方体）CNN模型，在模拟XMM-Newton观测数据上进行训练与对比；采用3D-GradCAM进行特征可视化与物理可解释性分析。

Result: 3D CNN在交叉验证中表现最稳定，能自主区分星系团热弥漫辐射与AGN非热幂律辐射；虽较2D+1D基线性能提升有限，但可隐式完成背景表征，无需人工背景扣除；直接基于原始光子计数操作，实现端到端自动化分类。

Conclusion: 3D事件立方体表示结合CNN是X射线源分类的有力范式，兼顾高性能、强鲁棒性、物理可解释性及流程简化，为大规模X射线星表自动分类提供了新路径。

Abstract: Distinguishing between faint extended X-ray sources is a fundamental yet challenging task in X-ray surveys. This paper presents a benchmark comparison of convolutional neural network models for classifying simulated XMM-Newton observations of AGN and galaxy clusters, focusing on the impact of data representation and interpretability of that data. We evaluate 1D, 2D, 2D+1D, and 3D CNNs trained on distinct data representations derived from simulated XMM-Newton observations of AGN and clusters: 1D (spectral), 2D (imaging) and 3D (event cube) data. We further apply 3D-GradCAM to interpret the learned features and assess the physical basis for the model's decision making to move beyond black-box classification. Our results demonstrate that increased spectral resolution enhances classifier performance using 1D and 2D information provides a strong baseline, the integration of both domains yields significant diagnostic gains. We find that a 3D CNN architecture applied directly to spatio-spectral event cubes provides the most robust performance, with superior consistency across cross-validation folds. Interpretability analysis reveals that the 3D model autonomously learns to distinguish the diffuse thermal emission of the intracluster medium from the localized, non-thermal power-law signatures of AGN. It exploits multi-dimensional correlations that are lost in lower-dimensional projections. While the performance gain over combined 2D and 1D baselines is marginal, the 3D approach demonstrates an ability to perform implicit background characterisation. By operating directly on total photon counts within event cubes, this method bypasses the need for manual background subtraction and modelling, offering a consistent, streamlined, end-to-end pipeline for automated classification of X-ray sources in large-scale catalogues.

</details>
